--- 10. Conditional Breakpoint Pattern ---
  In real code, use this pattern:

    for step in range(num_steps):
        loss = train_step(model, batch)
        if loss.item() > 10 or torch.isnan(loss):
            breakpoint()  # drops into pdb

  Useful pdb commands once inside:
    p tensor.shape       # print shape
    p tensor.device      # check device
    p tensor.grad        # inspect gradients
    p tensor.isnan().sum()  # count NaNs
    c                    # continue execution
    q                    # quit debugger
